{"type":"message_start","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I fixed the bug in `to_cents` in `ledger/currency.py`; all 5 tests now pass, including the full `tests/test_totals.py`.\n\n**Cause:** `to_cents` computed `int(amount * 100)`. Floats can't store most decimal prices exactly, so `19.99 * 100` comes out as `1998.999…`, and `int()` drops the fraction, giving 1998 instead of 1999. `0.29` lost a cent the same way.\n\n**Fix:** the amount now goes through `Decimal(str(amount))` before multiplying by 100, then rounds half-up to a whole number of cents. I used half-up rather than Python's built-in `round()` because `round()` sends exact halves to the even number.
